<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed ne Qatada se, unhone Hasan se, unhone Hattan bin Abdullah Raqashi se aur unhone Hazrat Ubada bin Samit (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki, unhone kaha: Nabi (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) par jab wahi nazil ki jati toh aap par uski wajah se takleef (ki kaifiyat) tari ho jati thi aur aapke chehre ka rang tabdeel ho jata tha, kaha: Ek din aap par wahi nazil ki gayi toh aap isi kaifiyat se dochar huye, jab aap se yeh kaifiyat door hui toh aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Mujhse seekh lo, Allah ne in (aurton) ke liye raah nikal di hai, shadi shuda, shadi shuda se (zina kare) aur kunwara, kunwari se (ya kunwara shadi shuda se toh) shadi shuda ke liye (saza) sau koray, phir pathron se rajam karna hai aur kunware ke liye sau koray phir ek saal ki jala-watni hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 4416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1748

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A4417)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 4417]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الْمُثَنَّى، وَابْنُ، بَشَّارٍ قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ جَعْفَرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَشَّارٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُعَاذُ بْنُ هِشَامٍ، حَدَّثَنِي أَبِي كِلاَهُمَا، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، بِهَذَا الإِسْنَادِ ‏.‏ غَيْرَ أَنَّ فِي، حَدِيثِهِمَا ‏ &quot;‏ الْبِكْرُ يُجْلَدُ وَيُنْفَى وَالثَّيِّبُ يُجْلَدُ وَيُرْجَمُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ لاَ يَذْكُرَانِ سَنَةً وَلاَ مِائَةً ‏</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). This own-language source explicitly supplies an abbreviated transmission or a reported wording variant. Its complete missing report and ultimate speaker are not reconstructed; named chain transmitters are not relabelled as speakers of omitted words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">This hadith has been reported on the authority of Qatada with the same chain of transmitters except with this variation that the unmarried is to be lashed and exiled, and the married one is to be lashed and stoned. There is neither any mention of one year nor that of one hundred</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 4417]</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Shu&#x27;bah aur Hisham ne Qatada se isi sanad ke sath hadees bayan ki, albatta un dono ki hadees mein hai: Kunware ko koray lagaye jayenge aur jala-watan kiya jayega aur shadi shuda ko koray lagaye jayenge aur rajam kiya jayega. Un dono ne (jala-watni ke liye) ek saal aur (koron ke liye) ek sau ka tazkira nahi kiya.</p>

## Narration 1749

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A4418)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Select only Umar&#x27;s explicitly embedded report of the Prophet applying stoning. Preserve the rest of Umar&#x27;s sermon, recollection of a verse, later community practice, concerns and legal discussion as separate source context. The whole sermon is not represented as the Prophet&#x27;s speech.</p>

<p dir="rtl">فَرَجَمَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 4418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 4418</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   4418 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي أَبُو الطَّاهِرِ، وَحَرْمَلَةُ بْنُ يَحْيَى، قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ وَهْبٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي يُونُسُ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، يَقُولُ قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَهُوَ جَالِسٌ عَلَى مِنْبَرِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِنَّ اللَّهَ قَدْ بَعَثَ مُحَمَّدًا صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالْحَقِّ وَأَنْزَلَ عَلَيْهِ الْكِتَابَ فَكَانَ مِمَّا أُنْزِلَ عَلَيْهِ آيَةُ الرَّجْمِ قَرَأْنَاهَا وَوَعَيْنَاهَا وَعَقَلْنَاهَا </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">وَرَجَمْنَا بَعْدَهُ فَأَخْشَى إِنْ طَالَ بِالنَّاسِ زَمَانٌ أَنْ يَقُولَ قَائِلٌ مَا نَجِدُ الرَّجْمَ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ فَيَضِلُّوا بِتَرْكِ فَرِيضَةٍ أَنْزَلَهَا اللَّهُ وَإِنَّ الرَّجْمَ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ حَقٌّ عَلَى مَنْ زَنَى إِذَا أَحْصَنَ مِنَ الرِّجَالِ وَالنِّسَاءِ إِذَا قَامَتِ الْبَيِّنَةُ أَوْ كَانَ الْحَبَلُ أَوْ الاِعْتِرَافُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 4418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 4418</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   4418 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah b. &#x27;Abbas reported that &#x27;Umar bin Khattab sat on the pulpit of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and said: Verily Allah sent Muhammad (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) with truth and He sent down the Book upon him, and the verse of stoning was included in what was sent down to him. We recited it, retained it in our memory and understood it. Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) awarded the punishment of stoning to death (to the married adulterer and adulteress) and, after him, we also awarded the punishment of stoning, I am afraid that with the lapse of time, the people (may forget it) and may say: We do not find the punishment of stoning in the Book of Allah, and thus go astray by abandoning this duty prescribed by Allah. Stoning is a duty laid down in Allah&#x27;s Book for married men and women who commit adultery when proof is established, or it there is pregnancy, or a confession</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 4418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 4418</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   4418 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यूनुस ने मुझे इब्न शिहाब से खबर दी, उन्होंने कहा: मुझे उबैदुल्लाह बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन उतबाह ने खबर दी के उन्होंने हज़रत अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से सुना, वह कह रहे थे: हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा, और वह रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के मिंबर पर तशरीफ़ फरमा थे: बिलाशुब्हा अल्लाह तआला ने मुहम्मद (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को हक़ के साथ मबऊस किया और आप पर किताब नाज़िल फरमाई, अल्लाह ने आप पर जो नाज़िल किया उसमें रज़्म की आयत भी थी, हमने उसे पढ़ा, याद किया और समझा, इसलिए रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने भी रज़्म की सज़ा दी और आप के बाद हमने भी रज़्म की सज़ा दी, मुझे डर है के लोगों पर एक लंबा ज़माना गुज़र जाएगा तो कोई कहने वाला कहेगा: हम अल्लाह की किताब में रज़्म (का हुकुम) नहीं पाते, तो वह लोग ऐसे फ़र्ज़ को छोड़ने से गुमराह हो जाएंगे जिसे अल्लाह ने नाज़िल किया है और बिलाशुब्हा अल्लाह की किताब में रज़्म (का हुकुम) औरतों और मर्दों में से हर एक पर जिसने ज़िना किया, जब वह शादी शुदा हो, बर-हक़ है। (यह सज़ा उस वक़्त दी जाएगी,) जब शहादत क़ायम हो जाए या हमल ठहर जाए या (ज़ानी की तरफ से) ऐतेराफ़ हो।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Select only Umar&#x27;s explicitly embedded report of the Prophet applying stoning. Preserve the rest of Umar&#x27;s sermon, recollection of a verse, later community practice, concerns and legal discussion as separate source context. The whole sermon is not represented as the Prophet&#x27;s speech.</p>

<p dir="rtl">اس لیے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے بھی رجم کی سزا دی </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 4418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 4418</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   4418 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یونس نے مجھے ابن شہاب سے خبر دی ، انہوں نے کہا : مجھے عبیداللہ بن عبداللہ بن عتبہ نے خبر دی کہ انہوں نے حضرت عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہ سے سنا ، وہ کہہ رہے تھے : حضرت عمر بن خطاب رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہا ، اور وہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے منبر پر تشریف فرما تھے : بلاشبہ اللہ تعالیٰ نے محمد صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو حق کے ساتھ مبعوث کیا اور آپ پر کتاب نازل فرمائی ، اللہ نے آپ پر جو نازل کیا اس میں رجم کی آیت بھی تھی ، ہم نے اسے پڑھا ، یاد کیا اور سمجھا ، </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">اور آپ نے بعد ہم نے بھی رجم کی سزا دی ، مجھے ڈر ہے کہ لوگوں پر ایک لمبا زمانہ گزر جائے گا تو کوئی کہنے والا کہے گا : ہم اللہ کی کتاب میں رجم ( کا حکم ) نہیں پاتے ، تو وہ لوگ ایسے فرض کو چھوڑنے سے گمراہ ہو جائیں گے جسے اللہ نے نازل کیا ہے اور بلاشبہ اللہ کی کتاب میں رجم ( کا حکم ) عورتوں اور مردوں میں سے ہر ایک پرجس نے زنا کیا ، جب وہ شادی شدہ ہو ، برحق ہے ۔ ( یہ سزا اس وقت دی جائے گی ، ) جب شہادت قائم ہو جائے یا حمل ٹھہر جائے یا ( زانی کی طرف سے ) اعتراف ہو</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   4418 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yunus ne mujhe Ibn Shihab se khabar di, unhone kaha: Mujhe Ubaidullah bin Abdullah bin Utbah ne khabar di ke unhone Hazrat Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se suna, woh keh rahe the: Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha, aur woh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke mimbar par tashreef farma the: Bilashuba Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne Muhammad (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko haq ke sath mab&#x27;oos kiya aur aap par kitab nazil farmayi, Allah ne aap par jo nazil kiya usme rajam ki aayat bhi thi, humne usey parha, yaad kiya aur samjha, isliye Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne bhi rajam ki saza di aur aap ke baad humne bhi rajam ki saza di, mujhe dar hai ke logon par ek lamba zamana guzar jayega toh koi kehne wala kahega: Hum Allah ki kitab mein rajam (ka hukum) nahi pate, toh woh log aise farz ko chhorne se gumrah ho jayenge jise Allah ne nazil kiya hai aur bilashuba Allah ki kitab mein rajam (ka hukum) aurton aur mardon mein se har ek par jisne zina kiya, jab woh shadi shuda ho, bar-haq hai. (Yeh saza us waqt di jayegi,) jab shahadat qaim ho jaye ya hamal thehar jaye ya (zani ki taraf se) aiteraf ho.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
